**The DEEP concept + why bigger.** ONE concept: *Karel's real recording exciting the WHOLE piano as 88 coupled damped resonators, so you SEE the sympathetic afterglow.* Model: 88 resonators (MIDI 21..108, `f_i=440·2^((midi-69)/12)`); precomputed coupling table `Score(i,j)=max_{p,q∈1..6} exp(-|f_i·p−f_j·q|/bw)` (octave/twelfth/fifth dominate); on his real onsets inject `velocity²` and spread a `Score`-weighted fraction to harmonic partners; two-regime damping (struck fast, sympathetic slow ~3s tail). DEEP picks the strongest implementation and folds the others' best ideas into the winner:
1. **"Concept and math are one" — the recallorbit/DEEP standard, decisive.** The resonancefield builder caught that a naive per-frame energy-*diffusion* coupling washes the whole keyboard to a uniform glow, and rewrote it (on both GPU and CPU) as **onset-impulse spreading** — each partner pulls a fraction of a struck string's *fresh onset* then rings down on its own tail — so a struck C lights its octave/twelfth/fifth as **distinct harmonic ridges** that linger ~3s while unrelated strings stay dark. Numerically verified selective + bounded. That selectivity is the whole point: it reads as *harmony*, not spectrum. The two others map the same model onto a bank/hall correctly but did not surface a physics correctness insight.

A single DEEP concept also discharges **#1** ("rest Canvas2D AND SVG-DOM — the new monoculture; ship a real GPU piece") and **#2** ("reclaim a live sensor — 11 of 15 are desk-input; mic/tilt/MIDI/camera all rested").

**Research-first (§1122, case b — continue a proven multi-cycle first).** RESEARCH §1122 (this cycle, same date): the live-2026 reservoir register has moved to **feature-free, raw-audio reservoirs whose ONLY trained part is a one-shot linear readout** — [Direct Raw Audio Signal Processing via Reservoir Computing (arXiv:2606.21335, Jun 2026)](https://arxiv.org/html/2606.21335) feeds raw waveforms straight into a fixed reservoir and trains only the readout; [Bridging Biological Hearing & Neuromorphic Computing (arXiv:2603.24283, Mar 2026)](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2603.24283) frames the same low-latency edge-audio reservoir. Both confirm a reservoir's learning is a **single ridge-regression readout fit** — no backprop, no ML libs. `10984-echofold` (§1114, the jury's 4/5 reservoir standout) used **fixed-random readouts by design** → it can only *drift*, never reproduce. The exact deepening the jury named = a **ridge-trained `Wout`** so the reservoir reproduces a phrase EXACTLY, with a DREAM knob morphing back to echofold's fixed-random dream. Chain today's research → today's build is visible.

**Ambition floor — honest, deliberately NO faked new-engine #1.** Greped BEFORE claiming: reservoir/ESN already exists (echofold), but a **trained** reservoir readout is **grep-0** (`ridge.?regress|tikhonov|trained.?readout` = 0 reservoir hits; the only ridge/least-squares hits are acoustics/tuning in `2392-room-tone`/`520-singing-dune`/`1930-harmonices`, none a readout). So #1 is the honest **trained-readout extension the jury named**, not a brand-new engine. `ambition (winner 11376-recallorbit): #1 + #2 + #3 + #4 + #5` (up to **5/5**, the jury's exact "take one first to 5/5" ask) — **#1** trained ridge readout (grep-0 for a trained reservoir readout); **#2** ≥5 subsystems (mic onset/centroid front-end + clock-conditioned ESN + ridge/Cholesky trainer + three.js phase-portrait render + FM-voice/drone synth); **#3** Jaeger 2001 + Lukoševičius–Jaeger 2009 (ridge readout training) named & ported; **#4** multi-cycle — cycle 2 of echofold's arc; **#5** RESEARCH §1122 (arXiv:2606.21335). The value is the *trained-readout deepening + verified exact recall*, exactly the "concentrate ambition on a proven first" the jury demanded.
